# Hole exercise: repair names

The source boundary is `26231a7` before the edit and the exercise tree committed as `d8db9f8` after it. Export snapshots were taken before and after the change in the isolated `hole-exercise` worktree. The comparison below uses the corrected closure from `7bf1bbe`, not the earlier journal's incomplete traversal.

Commands, run from the relevant `lean` directory:

```sh
LEAN_NUM_THREADS=2 lake exe journal export --out /Users/ember/dev/obend/astra/impl/hole-before.jsonl
LEAN_NUM_THREADS=2 lake exe journal export --out /Users/ember/dev/obend/astra/impl/hole-after.jsonl
LEAN_NUM_THREADS=2 lake exe journal diff /Users/ember/dev/obend/astra/impl/hole-before.jsonl /Users/ember/dev/obend/astra/impl/hole-after.jsonl --names
```

The exports and full diff, including each dead support, remain in local `astra/impl` scratch. This checked-in inventory preserves every name in the journal's changed-obligation and added/removed classes. Unaffected declarations and the repeated per-theorem dead-support lists are omitted. The classification counts are stated only in the headings below.

REVISED means the theorem's own recorded statement changed; STATEMENT-CITED and PROOF-CITED mean dependencies need re-checking. These are not counts of failed proofs or hand repairs. Changes to the Term inductive naturally reach many unchanged statements. Removed names below are generated equation/matcher/proof helpers; no authored theorem or pin was dropped.
